Function optimize(p, n, time, τ_schedule):
- p initialized normalized nonnegative.
- Maintain p_log maybe.
- For each step:
  - compute c and max.
  - update best.
  - compute q. Options: (1) q active softmax with temperature depending on plateau; (2) q mixture α uniform over top 50% + (1-α) dense softmax. We can tune.
  - compute grad = 2*corr(q,p) (scaled by Ncorr maybe). Use rfft.
  - normalize grad: e.g. scale = max(grad_abs) or norm. Use dir = -grad / scale.
  - Choose update type:
    a) Mirror: p_new = p * exp(-η * dir / p_avg? )
       If dir scaled O(1) and p avg 1/n, relative change ~exp(-η) if not multiplied by p. Use η ~0.01-1. This is similar previous.
    b) Projected gradient: p_new = project_simplex(p + step*dir). Step ~ c * n? Need line search.
  - Backtracking line search: try step sizes [η, η/2,...] maybe up to 10. Evaluate c quickly? But each eval costs FFT. Step generation multiple times expensive. Could use approximate linear decrease: predicted decrease = G? For nonconvex not. We can just try 2-3 steps per iteration.
  - Or use deterministic step with oscillation damping: p = (1-β)p + β p_new, β adapt (e.g. 0.1-1) to ensure not too much increase. If objective increases too much, revert.
- Anneal τ from high to low. High τ smooths; low focuses max. Could also periodically use "flatness penalty" d = c - max? Let's think.

Time-critical: we can implement optimize that within loop uses precomputed N, and computes c for current p. At start of iteration, we have c and maybe A? After update, recompute next. For line search, if we evaluate candidates, we need additional FFTs. Could be costly but okay if limited.

Maybe avoid line search by running small fixed steps and maintain best. Use momentum? Could be simpler and robust:
- For a phase, set eta schedule (e.g. start 0.5, decay to 0.01). Update b -= eta*h. p=softmax(b). Compute new max. Track best. Do not require monotone; just save best. If p becomes degenerate (sum issues), reset to best.
- To increase robustness, after each K steps if no improvement, reduce eta or add random noise / switch direction (variance).
- Since b can drift, center b periodically (subtract mean) to avoid large values.
At the end, polish with smaller eta and line search.

But if fixed step too large, p may become very spiky and max increases, but best kept. It may fail to descend from local. Better line search to ensure descent. Algorithm optimize_active(p, b, n, time_budget, tau_active, eta_init, min_eta, step_halves):

current F = eval p.
Loop while time left:
  c=full_convol(p) (if not have). max, mean.
  if max < best: save.
  compute q plateau/softmax. Need c.
  G = gradient_corr(q, p) (2*corr). This uses rfft q and p. But we already computed A for p for c: A=rfft(p,N). We can reuse A. Good: c_eval returns c and A. For grad, need Q=rfft(q,N); G=2*irfft(Q*conj(A),N)[:n]. That's 1 extra rfft Q and one irfft. So per iteration: 1 rfft p, 1 irfft c, 1 rfft q, 1 irfft grad = 4 transforms. Could combine? For q maybe dense; if active plateau sparse, could loop. But FFT ok.
  If G all zero break.
  h = G / max_abs(G). Center h: h -= h.mean(). Maybe also if h max not finite.
  eta = current.
  Do line search:
    candidates_eta = [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.125] maybe. But updating b for each candidate from same base: b_c = b - eta_c*h; p_c = softmax(b_c). Evaluate F_c. Choose best. We already have current. Do:

      for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
          if factor*eta < min_eta: break

          p_tmp = softmax stable
          F_tmp = only_max(eval)
          if F_tmp < best_F_cand - tol:

  Update eta: if accepted full factor maybe keep/increase; if only half reduce; if none reduce.

Softmax stable function:
def softmax_from_b(b):
    m=b.max()
    e=np.exp(b-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    return e/s

Active q computation:
def make_active_weights(c, cmax, cmean, active_frac, mix_soft, soft_temp_frac, min_active):
   L=len(c)
   tol = max(1e-13, active_frac*(cmax-cmean) + 1e-16)
   active = c >= cmax - tol
   count=active.sum()
   if count < min_active:
      # take top min_active indices
      idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:min_active]
      active = np.zeros(L,bool); active[idx]=True
      count=min_active
   q = active / count
   # Add soft tail maybe
   if mix_soft>0:
      # use temperature based on max-min? temp = max(soft_temp_frac*(cmax-cmean), 1e-16)
      # To avoid overflow, cmax used
      expw = np.exp((c - cmax)/temp)
      expw[~active]? Should include all. Normalize; for underflow maybe q_active dominates.
      q = (1-mix_soft)*q + mix_soft*expw/expw.sum()
   return q

   if cand_Max < cur_Max - 1e-15: True
   if allow_peak_increase and cand_Max <= cur_Max*(1+allow_peak_increase) and cand_loss < cur_loss - tol: True
   else False.

def resample_sequence(arr, n):
  arr=np.clip(arr,0,None); sum; if n==len return arr/sum.
  xs_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(arr)); xs_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); vals=np.log(np.maximum(arr/sum,1e-300)); interp=np.exp(np.interp(xs_new,xs_old,vals)-np.max(...)); normalize.

Normalization:
def make_positive_normalized(x, n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x.size != n: maybe trim/pad/resample.
    x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: x=np.ones(n)/n
    else: x=x/s
    return x

Base:
def make_base(n):
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1):
       a[i+1]=a[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
    return a/a.sum()
Recurrence: for i from 0 to n-2: a[i+1]=a[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2). Test i=0 =>1*1/2=0.5; yes. Complexity O(n) Python loop for n=16384 fine. Could vectorize cumulative product: ratio = (2*arange(n-1)+1)/(2*arange(n-1)+2); a[1:]=cumprod(ratio). Use numpy vectorized:
    idx=np.arange(1,n,dtype=float)
    ratios=(2*idx-1)/(2*idx)  # for a_i from a_{i-1}: i=1 ratio=1/2; i=2 ratio=3/4; yes
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1; a[1:]=np.cumprod(ratios)
Normalize.

Resample:
def resample_to_length(x,n):
    if len==n return normalize.
    x=normalize; if x.size<2 return ones.
    logv=np.log(np.maximum(x,1e-300)); old=np.linspace(0,1,x.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n); vals=np.exp(np.interp(new,old,logv)); return normalize(vals,n)

def eval_sequence_fft(p, N, Lc):
    A = rfft(p, N)
    c = irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[:Lc]
    maxc = float(c.max())
    return c, A, maxc

A remains complex.

def smooth_loss(c, T, cmean):
    # T >0
    z = (c - cmean) / T
    zmax = z.max()
    # to avoid underflow, if zmax large, subtract
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <=0: return c.max() # fallback
    return T * (zmax + np.log(s))
But this allocates e. We already have c; for each candidate, computing loss adds O(n) and allocation. If we don't use loss for acceptance, skip. Maybe use var (np.dot(c,c)) less allocation? dot no new array. Use var_t = float(np.dot(c_t, c_t)) - 1/Lc^2. np.dot on float array length 2n-1 no big allocation. Good.
Acceptance: cand_max < cur_max or (cand_max <= cur_max*(1+allow) and cand_var < cur_var*(1-1e-8)). This avoids logsum.

Center/scale h:
def center_scale(h):
   if h.size==0: return None
   h = h - h.mean()
   hmin = h.min(); hmax = h.max(); rng = hmax-hmin
   if not finite or rng <= 1e-18: return None
   h = h / (0.5*rng)
   # If huge due outliers, clip? h now max=1, min=-1 if exact. Due float maybe.
   return h

Implement make_q(c, T_frac):
   cmax=c.max(); cmean=1/Lc or c.mean (fft sum ~1). diff=max(cmax-cmean, tinies)
   T=max(T_frac*diff, tinies)
   # active:
   tol=max(1e-15, 0.02*diff) # maybe 0.02 or 0.05
   thresh=cmax-tol
   excess=np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)
   s_ex=excess.sum()
   if s_ex > 0:
      q_act = excess/s_ex
   else:
      q_act = np.full(Lc,1/Lc); q_act=np.maximum(c,0); if sum... uniform top min_active?
   # To ensure min active count, if active_count < min_active, top indices:
   active_count=(c>=thresh).sum()
   if active_count < 32:
      idx=np.argsort(c)[-32:]; q_act=np.zeros(Lc); q_act[idx]=1/32
   # soft:
   z=(c-cmax)/T
   e=np.exp(z)  # max 1, no overflow
   se=e.sum()
   if se>0: q_soft=e/se
   else q_soft=q_act
   # blend. If active_count large and q_act uniform, soft similar.
   mix = 0.25 if T_frac >0.1 else 0.1? Maybe use 0.2.
   q=(1-mix)*q_act + mix*q_soft
   normalize.

def grad_corr(q,p,A,N,n):
    Q=rfft(q,N)
    g=irfft(Q*np.conj(A), N)
    g = g[:n]
    # Due numerical, g can negative; clip small negative? If q,p nonnegative, g should nonnegative. But after soft q positive. Use np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g) maybe; But if numerical negative significant? not. Clip to 0.
    np.clip(g,0.0,None,out=g)
    return 2.0*g

def make_q(c, T_frac, Lc):
   cmax = c.max()
   cmean = 1.0/Lc  # or c.mean but sum maybe 1; use Lc exact for stability
   diff = cmax - cmean
   tiny = max(1e-300, np.finfo(float).eps*max(1.0,cmax)*10)
   if diff <= tiny:
       q = np.full(Lc, 1.0/Lc); return q
   T = max(T_frac*diff, tiny)
   # active
   tol = max(1e-15, 0.03*diff)
   thresh = cmax - tol
   excess = np.maximum(c - thresh, 0.0)  # alloc
   exsum = excess.sum()
   if exsum > 0:
      q_act = excess / exsum
      # If too few active? Check active count:
      count = int(np.count_nonzero(c >= thresh))
   else: q_act uniform
   if count < 32:
      idx = np.argsort(c)[-32:] # descending
      q_act = np.zeros(Lc); q_act[idx]=1.0/32
   # soft
   z = (c - cmax)/T
   # z max 0, min maybe large negative; exp safe
   e = np.exp(z)
   se = e.sum()
   if se > 0:
      q_soft = e / se
   else: q_soft = q_act
   # Mix. If T_frac small, less soft? Let's set mix = min(0.3, max(0.05, T_frac))
   mix = 0.15
   q = (1.0 - mix)*q_act + mix*q_soft
   s = q.sum()
   if s>0: q /= s
   else: q = np.full(Lc,1/Lc)
   return q
```

If tail values close, active includes tail with excess smaller? Actually c-thresh from 0 to tol; tail near threshold weight small. Good.

def center_scale(h):
   h = h - h.mean()
   hmax = h.max(); hmin = h.min(); rng=hmax-hmin
   if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng <= 1e-18: return None
   out = h / (0.5*rng)
   # optional clip to [-4,4] to prevent overflow
   np.clip(out, -4.0, 4.0, out=out)
   # after clip, maybe recenter? not needed
   return out

def update_p(p,h,eta):
   # h range about [-1,1]
   f = np.exp(-eta*h)
   p2 = p*f
   s=p2.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return None
   p2 /= s
   return p2

def optimize_sequence(p_init, n, N, Lc, time_limit, T_frac, eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng):
    p = normalize_pos(p_init,n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    c, A, maxc = eval_main(p,N,Lc)
    varc = float(np.dot(c,c)) - (1.0/Lc)**2
    best_p = p.copy(); best_max=maxc; best_var=varc; best_F=2*n*best_max
    cur_max=maxc; cur_var=varc
    eta = eta_init
    fail=0
    iters=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
       iters+=1
       # occasionally if far from best reset?
       q = make_q(c, T_frac, Lc)
       G = grad_corr(q,p,A,N,n)
       h = center_scale(G)
       if h is None: break
       trials_best = None
       # evaluate full
       eta_try=eta
       if eta_try > max_eta: eta_try=max_eta

```
def generate_initials(n,rng):
   inits=[]
   base=make_base(n)
   inits.append(base)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for th in (0.2,0.5,1.0,-0.2,-0.5): inits.append(base*np.exp(th*x))
   for beta,gam in [(0.2,0.5),(0.5,1),(1.0,0.5),(1.0,2),(0.3,2)]: inits.append(base*(1+beta*x)**gam)
   # random: base * exp(0.3*rng.normal(size=n) smoothed?) Use cumulative? Simple lognormal:
   w=np.exp(0.2*rng.normal(0,1,n)); inits.append(base*w)
   # smooth random: use rng normal then moving average? Too much. Use sine phases:
   for ph in (0.5,1.5): inits.append(base*(1+0.2*np.sin(2*np.pi*2*x+ph)+0.1*np.sin(2*np.pi*5*x)))
   # previous
   prev=get_prev()
   if prev:
      inits.append(resample_sequence(prev,n))
   return inits
```

Therefore make_q simple:
```
def make_q(c,T_frac):
   cmax=c.max(); cmean=1/Lc; diff=max(cmax-cmean, tinies); T=max(T_frac*diff,tinies)
   z=(c-cmax)/T
   e=np.exp(z)  # max 1, min 0
   s=e.sum()
   if s>0 and finite: e/=s; return e
   return np.full(Lc,1/Lc)
```
This saves code.

Let's detail `optimize_sequence` with no lambdas and efficient candidate selection.
```
def optimize_sequence(p_init, n, N, Lc, time_budget, T_frac, eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng):
    p = normalize_pos(p_init, n)
    if time_budget <= 1: return p, 2.0*n*eval_max_only(p,N,Lc)
    start=time.monotonic()
    c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p,N,Lc)
    cur_var = compute_var(c, Lc)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_max=cur_max; best_var=cur_var
    eta=eta_init
    fail=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_budget:
        # if too far from best and no fail? maybe if cur_max > best_max*1.05 reset
        if cur_max > best_max*1.05 + 1e-15:
            p=best_p.copy(); c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc); cur_var=...
        q=make_q(c,T_frac,Lc)
        G=grad_corr(q,p,A,N,n)
        h=center_scale(G)
        if h is None:
            break
        # evaluate full
        eta_full = min(eta, max_eta)
        p_try = scale_normalize(p,h,eta_full)
        if p_try is None:
            eta=max(min_eta, eta*0.5); fail+=1; continue
        max_try, var_try, A_try = eval_max_var(p_try,N,Lc)
        accepted=False
        accepted_p=None
        # full acceptable?
        if max_better(max_try,cur_max) or (allow_peak>0 and max_try <= cur_max*(1+allow_peak) and var_try < cur_var*(1-1e-9)):
            # if improvement not huge, try half to see better?
            if cur_max - max_try <= 1e-8*max(1.0,cur_max) or allow_peak>0:
                # evaluate half and quarter choosing best
                trials=[(max_try,p_try)]
                eta_half=eta_full*0.5
                if eta_half>=min_eta:
                   ph=scale_normalize(p,h,eta_half); if ... eval; trials append
                   # maybe if half max much better than full, choose.
                eta_q=eta_full*0.25; if needed...
                choose best among trials that accept? If no accept, maybe choose best neutral? We didn't accept; but if full acceptable, accepted true.
            else:
                accepted_p=(max_try,var_try,p_try,A_try); accepted=True
        if not accepted:
            # try half, then quarter
            for factor in (0.5,0.25):
                e=eta_full*factor
                if e<min_eta: break
                pt=scale_normalize(p,h,e); if none continue
                mt,vt,At=eval_max_var; 
                if max_better(mt,cur_max) or (allow_peak>0 and ...):
                    accepted=True; accepted_p=(...); # Could still be full? full not accepted, half is. break? Maybe quarter better? break for speed.
                    break
        if accepted:
            cur_max,cur_var,p,A = accepted_p[0],...; c=irfft(A*A,N)[:Lc] clipped
            fail=0
            if cur_max < best_max:
                best_max, best_p, best_var = cur_max, p.copy(), cur_var
            # adapt eta: if full accepted maybe increase
            if accepted_full_flag: eta = min(max_eta, eta*1.05)
            else: # smaller accepted
                # if factor 0.5, decrease slight
            continue
        # no accept
        fail +=1
        eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.7)
        # if fail big: restart from best with perturbation
        if fail > 150:
           p=best_p.copy()
           noise=rng.normal(0.0,0.08,n)
           p = p*np.exp(noise); p=normalize_pos(p,n)
           c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc); cur_var=...
           if cur_max < best_max: update (unlikely)
           eta = eta_init
           fail=0
        if fail > 800:
           break
    # After loop, best_p maybe; return best_p, 2n*best_max
```

Candidate selection without lambda:
```
def trial_accepts(m_new, v_new, m_cur, v_cur, allow_peak):
    if m_new < m_cur - 1e-14: return True
    if allow_peak > 0.0 and m_new <= m_cur*(1.0+allow_peak) and v_new < v_cur*(1.0-1e-9): return True
    return False
```
```
def better_trial(m1,v1,m2,v2):
    if m1 < m2 - 1e-15: return 1
    if m2 < m1 - 1e-15: return 2
    return 1 if v1 < v2 else 2
```

def make_c_from_A(A,N,Lc):
    c_full = irfft(A*A,N)
    c = c_full[:Lc]

`scale_normalize(p,h,eta)`:
```
def scale_normalize(p,h,eta):
    # h centered, roughly [-1,1]
    # To guard overflow, clip exponent to [-50,50]? h*eta maybe <=2; no need but do:
    eh = -eta*h
    # if eh max > 500, scale? But not.
    f=np.exp(eh)
    p2=p*f
    s=p2.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    p2 /= s
    return p2
```

def get_previous_sequence():
    obj = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if obj is None: return None
    try: arr=np.asarray(obj,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    except: return None
    if arr.size<2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None
    return arr

Let's design generate_initials more targeted:
```
def generate_initials(n, rng, prev):
   base=make_base(n)
   base_rev=base[::-1]
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   cands=[]
   cands.append(base)
   cands.append(base_rev)
   for b in (base,base_rev):
      for th in (-0.5,0.5): cands.append(b*np.exp(th*x))
      for m,ph in ((2,0.5),(4,2.0),(8,1.0)):
          mod=1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + ph)
          cands.append(b*mod)
   # maybe amplitude modulation off: b*(1+0.5*np.cos(2π*4*x))
   # random lognormal and smooth random
   cands.append(base*np.exp(0.2*rng.normal(0,1,n)))
   if prev: cands.append(resample_sequence(prev,n))
   # Also a pure smooth non-base initial: normalized (1+0.3*sin(2π*3*x+1))**2 maybe? But base good.
   return cands
```
Normalize each in scan; if nonnegative becomes zero for mod? mod min 0.5 positive.

Pseudo propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=None):
    if budget_s is None: budget_s=2300.0
    budget_s=float(budget_s)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total_deadline=t0 + min(budget_s, 2350.0)
    safety=30.0
    hard_deadline=total_deadline-safety
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p=None; best_F=np.inf
    prev=get_previous_sequence()
    if prev is not None:
       p=normalize_pos(prev, prev.size); n=p.size
       if 16 <= n <= 20000:
          N=next_fft_len_for_n(n); Lc=2*n-1
          maxc=eval_max_only(p,N,Lc); F=2.0*n*maxc
          if F<best_F: best_p=p; best_F=F
          print(...)
    # Budgets based on remaining
    rem=hard_deadline-time.monotonic()
    # We want maybe 55% for 8192, 45% for 16384.
    b8192 = max(120, 0.55*rem)
    dead8192 = time.monotonic()+b8192
    if dead8192 < hard_deadline:
       n8=8192
       p8,F8=search_length(n8, dead8192, rng, prev, best_p,best_F)
       if F8<best_F: best_p,best_F=p8,F8
    # For 16384 if time
    rem=hard_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 300:
       b16384 = min(rem-30, 0.6*rem?) # use most but leave final
       dead16384=time.monotonic()+b16384
       p16,F16=search_length(16384, dead16384, rng, prev, best_p,best_F)
       update
    # Final polish global best
    rem=hard_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60 and best_p is not None:
       n=len(best_p); N=...; Lc...
       p_pol, F_pol = optimize_sequence(best_p,n,N,Lc,max(10,rem-10), T_frac=0.03, eta_init=0.15, min_eta=1e-5, max_eta=0.5, allow_peak=0.0, rng=rng)
       if 2*n*F? optimize returns F maybe. Need signature return p,F (already objective). Let's have optimize_sequence return (best_p, best_F=2n*best_max). Then pass.
    # optional evaluate_sequence for small n
    return list.
```
But search_length itself optimizes using stages and returns best_F. It should also take current best to potentially update? We can just let it return local best; propose updates.

`search_length` deadline handling:
```
def search_length(n, deadline, rng, prev, best_p,best_F):
    start=time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= start+20: return best_p,best_F
    budget=deadline-start
    N=...; Lc=...
    inits=generate_initials(n,rng,prev)
    scored=[]
    for p in inits:
       p=normalize_pos(p,n)
       if p.sum()<=0: continue

Detailed function code:

def next_fft_len_for_n(n):
    if n <= 1: return 1
    return int(next_fast_len(3*n - 2))

Can `next_fast_len` return numpy int? int.

`normalize_pos`:
```
def normalize_pos(x, n):
    arr=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < n:
       # pad zeros
       out=np.zeros(n); out[:arr.size]=arr; arr=out
    elif arr.size > n:
       arr=arr[:n]
    else: arr=arr.copy()
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.maximum(arr,0.0,out=arr)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       arr.fill(1.0/n)
    else:
       arr *= 1.0/s
    return arr
```
If n=0? not.

`make_base`:
```
def make_base(n):
    if n == 1: return np.array([1.0])
    idx=np.arange(1,n,dtype=np.float64)
    ratios=(2.0*idx - 1.0)/(2.0*idx)
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0]=1.0
    a[1:]=np.cumprod(ratios)
    s=a.sum()
    a /= s
    return a
```
For large n cumprod no underflow? ratios ~1, product ~1/√n ~0.005 for n=32768, fine.

`resample_sequence` as above.

def _clean_c(c_view):
    np.maximum(c_view, 0.0, out=c_view)
    return c_view

c = np.clip(c, 0.0, None)  # creates new array

```
def _convolution_to_c(p_or_A, N,Lc):
   if p is array: A=rfft(p,N)
   else: A=p
   c_full=irfft(A*A,N)
   c=np.asarray(c_full[:Lc], dtype=np.float64).copy()
   np.clip(c,0.0,None,out=c)
   return c,A if needed
```
Copy ensures no full retention. Time small. For eval_max_var, copy c then dot, then c freed. Fine.

def eval_main(p,N,Lc):
    A=rfft(p,N)
    c_full=irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c_full[:Lc].copy()

```

`make_q(c,T_frac,Lc)`:
```
def make_q(c, T_frac, Lc):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    cmean=1.0/Lc
    diff=cmax-cmean
    tiny=1e-300
    if diff <= tiny:
       return np.full(Lc,1.0/Lc)
    T=max(float(T_frac)*diff, tiny)
    z=(c-cmax)/T
    # z max 0. Clip z to [-700,0] to avoid underflow? np.exp handles underflow no warning maybe; to be safe:
    z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(Lc,1.0/Lc)
    e /= s
    return e
```
If T very small, c-cmax may be negative large; clip to -700. Good.
If c contains values slightly >cmax? cmax max, no. z<=0.

`grad_corr`:
```
def grad_corr(q,p,A,N,n):
    Q=rfft(q,N)
    g=irfft(Q*np.conjugate(A),N)
    g=g[:n].copy()
    np.clip(g,0.0,None,out=g)
    g *= 2.0
    return g
```
Need Q*conj(A) allocates complex length N/2+1; okay. Could use `np.multiply(Q, np.conjugate(A))`. Fine.

`center_scale`:
```
def center_scale(h):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return None
    h = h - float(h.mean())
    hmax=float(h.max()); hmin=float(h.min())
    rng=hmax-hmin
    if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng <= 1e-18: return None
    out = h / (0.5*rng)
    # out max ~1 min ~-1; clip
    np.clip(out, -5.0, 5.0, out=out)
    return out
```

`scale_normalize`:
```
def scale_normalize(p,h,eta):
    if eta <= 0: return p.copy()
    eh = -eta*h
    # protect
    if eh.min() < -700 or eh.max() > 700: # should not
        eh = np.clip(eh, -700.0, 700.0)
    f=np.exp(eh)
    p2=p*f
    s=float(p2.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    p2 /= s
    return p2
```

def better_trial_index(m_new,v_new,f_new,m_cur,v_cur): # only if better
```

`optimize_sequence` full code. Let's draft:
```
def optimize_sequence(p_init, n, N, Lc, time_budget, T_frac, eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng):
    p = normalize_pos(p_init, n)
    start = time.monotonic()
    c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p,N,Lc)
    cur_var = compute_var(c,Lc)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_max = cur_max
    best_var = cur_var
    best_F = 2.0*float(n)*best_max
    eta = max(min_eta, min(eta_init, max_eta))
    fail = 0
    if time_budget <= 0: return best_p,best_F
    while time.monotonic() - start < time_budget:
        # If current is badly worse than best, reset to best
        if best_max > 0 and cur_max > best_max*(1.0+0.03) + 1e-15:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c,A,cur_max = eval_main(p,N,Lc)
            cur_var = compute_var(c,Lc)
            fail=0
        q = make_q(c,T_frac,Lc)
        G = grad_corr(q,p,A,N,n)
        h = center_scale(G)
        if h is None:
            break
        # full trial
        eta_full = min(eta, max_eta)
        if eta_full < min_eta:
            break
        trials=[]
        p_full = scale_normalize(p,h,eta_full)
        if p_full is not None:
            m1,v1,A1 = eval_max_var(p_full,N,Lc)
            accept1 = (m1 < cur_max - 1e-15) or (allow_peak>0 and m1 <= cur_max*(1.0+allow_peak) and v1 < cur_var*(1.0-1e-9))
            # choose best among accepted. We'll consider full if accepted; maybe if improvement small evaluate half.
            if accept1:
                # if improvement is very significant, take full without half
                if cur_max - m1 > 1e-7*max(1.0, cur_max):
                    best_trial = (m1,v1,p_full,A1,1.0)
                    accepted = True
                else:
                    # evaluate half
                    accepted = False
                    best_trial = (m1,v1,p_full,A1,1.0)
                    e_half = eta_full*0.5
                    if e_half >= min_eta:
                       p_h=scale_normalize(p,h,e_half)
                       if p_h is not None:
                          mh,vh,Ah=eval_max_var(p_h,N,Lc)
                          if (mh < cur_max - 1e-15) or (allow_peak>0 and ...):
                              # choose better of full and half
                              if mh < m1 - 1e-15 or (abs(mh-m1)<=1e-15 and vh < v1): best_trial=(mh,vh,p_h,Ah,0.5)
                    # maybe quarter if no accepted? full accepted so accepted true.
            else:
                # full not accepted, try half then quarter
                accepted=False; best_trial=None
                for fact in (0.5,0.25,0.125):
                   e=eta_full*fact
                   if e<min_eta: break
                   pt=scale_normalize(p,h,e)
                   if pt is None: continue
                   mt,vt,At=eval_max_var(pt,N,Lc)
                   if (mt < cur_max - 1e-15) or (allow_peak>0 and mt <= cur_max*(1+allow_peak) and vt < cur_var*(1-1e-9)):
                       accepted=True; best_trial=(mt,vt,pt,At,fact); break
        else:
            accepted=False; best_trial=None
        if accepted and best_trial is not None:
            cur_max,cur_var,p,A,fact = best_trial[:5]
            # Recompute c from A for next
            c_full = irfft(A*A,N)
            c = c_full[:Lc].copy()
            np.clip(c,0.0,None,out=c)
            fail=0
            if cur_max < best_max - 1e-18:
                best_max=cur_max; best_p=p.copy(); best_var=cur_var; best_F=2.0*n*best_max
                print? maybe not every step
            if fact >= 0.9:
                eta = min(max_eta, eta*1.05)
            elif fact <= 0.25:
                eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.9)
            else:
                # half
                # if half accepted but full not, keep eta maybe 0.95
                eta = max(min_eta, min(max_eta, eta*0.95))
            continue
        # no accept
        fail += 1
        eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.7)
        if fail > 100:
            # restart from best with multiplicative noise
            p = best_p.copy()
            sigma = 0.08
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            p = p * np.exp(noise)
            p = normalize_pos(p,n)
            c,A,cur_max = eval_main(p,N,Lc)
            cur_var = compute_var(c,Lc)
            if cur_max < best_max:
                best_max=cur_max; best_p=p.copy(); best_var=cur_var; best_F=2.0*n*best_max
            eta = max(min_eta, eta*1.5)
            fail = 0
        if eta <= min_eta*1.01 and fail > 30:
            break
        if fail > 1500:
            break
    return best_p, best_F
```

   ...

Let's write `generate_initials(n,rng,prev)` with top-level.
```
def generate_initials(n, rng, prev):
   base=make_base(n)
   rev=base[::-1].copy()
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   cands=[]
   cands.append(base)
   cands.append(rev)
   for b in (base, rev):
      cands.append(b*np.exp(-0.4*x))
      cands.append(b*np.exp(0.4*x))
      cands.append(b*(1.0 + 0.3*np.sin(2*np.pi*3.0*x + 0.4)))
   # Add base modulations without reverse maybe
   cands.append(base*(1.0 + 0.4*np.cos(2*np.pi*4.0*x)))
   cands.append(base*(1.0 + 0.3*np.sin(2*np.pi*8.0*x + 1.2)))
   cands.append(base*np.exp(0.25*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)))
   if prev is not None and prev.size >= 16:
       cands.append(resample_sequence(prev,n))
   # Also a pure base * exp smooth tilt? Already.
   return cands
```
Number 11. Good.
Need ensure mod positive: 1+0.3 sin min .7, good. 1+0.4 cos min .6. Good.

`trial_accepts`:
```
def trial_accepts(m_new, v_new, m_cur, v_cur, allow_peak):
    if m_new < m_cur - 1e-15:
        return True
    if allow_peak > 0.0:
        if m_new <= m_cur * (1.0 + allow_peak) and v_new < v_cur * (1.0 - 1e-9):
            return True
    return False
```
If v_cur <=0? Variance should positive. If negative small, `(1 - 1e-9)` more negative, maybe never. Use also `v_new < v_cur - 1e-14`. For small var ~1e-9, relative ok. Good.

`trial_better`:
```
def trial_better(m1,v1,m2,v2):
    if m1 < m2 - 1e-15: return True
    if m2 < m1 - 1e-15: return False
    return v1 < v2
```

Pseudo final optimize:
```
def optimize_sequence(...):
    p = normalize_pos(p_init,n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc)
    cur_var=compute_var(c,Lc)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_max=cur_max; best_var=cur_var; best_F=2*n*best_max
    eta=...
    fail=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_budget:
       it +=1
       if it % 1000 ==0: print(...)
       if cur_max > best_max*(1+0.03)+1e-15:
          p=best_p.copy(); c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc); cur_var=...
       q=make_q(c,T_frac,Lc)
       G=grad_corr(q,p,A,N,n)
       h=center_scale(G)
       if h is None:
          fail +=1
          if fail > 100: break
          p=best_p.copy(); noise=...; p=...; c,A,cur_max=...; cur_var=...; eta=...; fail=0; continue
       eta_full=min(eta,max_eta)
       if eta_full < min_eta: break
       accepted=False; best_m=None; ...
       pf=scale_normalize(...)
       if pf is not None:
          mf,vf,Af,cf=eval_max_var(...)
          if trial_accepts(mf,vf,cur_max,cur_var,allow_peak):
              accepted=True; set full
              if cur_max - mf <= 1e-8*max(cur_max,1e-30):
                 # try half/quarter
                 for fact in (0.5,0.25):
                    ...
          else:
             for fact in (0.5,0.25,0.125):
                ...
                if accepted: break
       if accepted:
          p=best_ptr; cur_max=best_m; cur_var=best_v; A=best_A; c=best_c
          fail=0
          if cur_max < best_max - 1e-18:
             best_max=cur_max; best_p=p.copy(); best_var=cur_var; best_F=2n best
          # adapt
          if best_fact > 0.9: eta=min(max_eta, eta*1.05)
          elif best_fact > 0.4: eta=max(min_eta, eta*0.98)
          else: eta=max(min_eta, eta*0.85)
       else:
          fail +=1
          eta=max(min_eta, eta*0.75)
          if fail > 100:
             p=best_p.copy(); noise=...; p=...; eval; if cur_max < best update; eta=max(min_eta, eta_init*0.8); fail=0
          if eta <= min_eta*1.02 and fail > 30: break
          if fail > 2000: break
    return best_p,best_F
```

def run_stage(p, n, N, Lc, deadline_stage, T_frac, eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng, tag):
    tl = deadline_stage - time.monotonic()
    if tl < 2.0:
        return p, 2.0*float(n)*eval_max_only(p,N,Lc)
    return optimize_sequence(p, n, N, Lc, tl, T_frac, eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng, tag)
```

def optimize_sequence(...):
    p = normalize_pos(p_init, n)
    start = time.monotonic()
    c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p, N, Lc)
    cur_var = compute_var(c, Lc)
    best_seq = p.copy()
    best_max = cur_max
    best_var = cur_var
    best_F = 2.0 * float(n) * best_max
    eta = float(eta_init)
    if eta > max_eta: eta = float(max_eta)
    if eta < min_eta: eta = float(min_eta)
    fail=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() - start < float(time_budget):
       it +=1
       if it % 750 ==0:
          print(...)
       if best_max > 0.0 and cur_max > best_max * 1.03 + 1e-15:
          p=best_seq.copy()
          c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc)
          cur_var=compute_var(c,Lc)
          fail=0
       q=make_q(c, T_frac, Lc)
       G=grad_corr(q, p, A, N, n)
       h=center_scale(G)
       if h is None:
          fail +=1
          if fail > 80: break
          p = perturb_sequence(best_seq, 0.08, rng)
          c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc)
          cur_var=compute_var(c,Lc)
          if cur_max < best_max:
             best_max=...
          eta = max(min_eta, min(max_eta, float(eta_init)*0.7))
          fail=0
          continue
       eta_full = min(eta, max_eta)
       if eta_full < min_eta: break
       accepted=False
       best_m = None; best_v = None; best_p_try=None; best_A_try=None; best_c_try=None; best_fact=1.0
       p_try = scale_normalize(p, h, eta_full)
       if p_try is not None:
           m_try, v_try, A_try, c_try = eval_max_var(p_try, N, Lc)
           full_ok = trial_accepts(m_try, v_try, cur_max, cur_var, allow_peak)
           factors = (0.5, 0.25)
           if full_ok:
               accepted=True; best_m=m_try; best_v=v_try; best_p_try=p_try; best_A_try=A_try; best_c_try=c_try; best_fact=1.0
               improve = cur_max - m_try
               if m_try < cur_max and improve <= 1e-8 * max(cur_max, 1e-300):
                   factors=(0.5,0.25,0.125)
               else:
                   factors=()
           else:
               factors=(0.5,0.25,0.125)
           for fact in factors:
               e = eta_full * fact
               if e < min_eta: continue
               pt2=scale_normalize(p,h,e)
               if pt2 is None: continue
               m2,v2,A2,c2=eval_max_var(pt2,N,Lc)
               if trial_accepts(m2,v2,cur_max,cur_var,allow_peak):
                   if not accepted or trial_better(m2,v2,best_m,best_v):
                       accepted=True; best_m=m2; best_v=v2; best_p_try=pt2; best_A_try=A2; best_c_try=c2; best_fact=fact
       if accepted and best_p_try is not None:
           p = best_p_try; cur_max = best_m; cur_var=best_v; A=best_A_try; c=best_c_try
           fail=0
           if cur_max < best_max - 1e-18:
               best_max=cur_max; best_seq=p.copy(); best_var=cur_var; best_F=2.0*float(n)*best_max
           if best_fact > 0.9:
               eta = min(max_eta, eta*1.06)
           elif best_fact > 0.4:
               eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.98)
           else:
               eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.85)
           # eta could exceed max if increase; min handles
       else:
           fail +=1
           eta = max(min_eta, eta*0.72)
           if fail > 80:
               p = perturb_sequence(best_seq, 0.07, rng)
               c,A,cur_max=eval_main(p,N,Lc)
               cur_var=compute_var(c,Lc)
               if cur_max < best_max:
                  ...
               eta = max(min_eta, min(max_eta, float(eta_init)*0.75))
               fail=0
           if eta <= min_eta*1.02 and fail > 30:
               break
           if fail > 1500:
               break
    return best_seq, best_F

```python
"""
Search non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

by working with L1-normalized sequences (sum = 1), so the objective is
2*n*max(conv(a,a)).

The method starts from several high-quality initializations, including the
global variable height_sequence_1 when present and a central-binomial
construction whose infinite-square has almost flat coefficients.  It then
runs smooth multiplicative-weight projected gradient searches with a
temperature schedule.  The gradient is the standard gradient of a smoothed
max of the convolution and is evaluated by FFT for speed.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len


def next_fft_len_for_n(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return int(next_fast_len(3 * n - 2))


def normalize_pos(x, n):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < n:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        out[:arr.size] = arr
        arr = out
    elif arr.size > n:
        arr = arr[:n].copy()
    else:
        arr = arr.copy()

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        arr.fill(1.0 / float(n))
    else:
        arr *= (1.0 / s)
    return arr


def make_base(n):
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    ratios = (2.0 * idx - 1.0) / (2.0 * idx)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    a[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a[:] = 1.0 / n
    else:
        a /= s
    return a


def resample_sequence(x, n):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = arr.sum()
    if arr.size != n:
        arr = normalize_pos(arr, arr.size)
        if arr.size < 2:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        logv = np.log(np.maximum(arr, 1e-300))
        vals = np.exp(np.interp(new_x, old_x, logv))
        return normalize_pos(vals, n)
    return normalize_pos(arr, n)


def get_previous_sequence():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 2:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    if arr.sum() <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def eval_max_only(p, N, Lc):
    A = rfft(p, N)
    c_full = irfft(A * A, N)
    return float(np.max(c_full[:Lc]))


def eval_main(p, N, Lc):
    A = rfft(p, N)
    c_full = irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c_full[:Lc].copy()
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    return c, A, maxc


def eval_max_var(p, N, Lc):
    A = rfft(p, N)
    c_full = irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c_full[:Lc].copy()
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    varc = compute_var(c, Lc)
    return maxc, varc, A, c


def compute_var(c, Lc):
    mean = 1.0 / float(Lc)
    v = float(np.dot(c, c) - mean * mean)
    if v < 0.0 and v > -1e-20:
        v = 0.0
    return v


def make_q(c, T_frac, Lc):
    cmax = float(c.max())
    cmean = 1.0 / float(Lc)
    diff = cmax - cmean
    if diff <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(Lc, 1.0 / float(Lc), dtype=np.float64)
    T = max(float(T_frac) * float(diff), 1e-300)
    z = (c - cmax) / T
    np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0, out=z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(Lc, 1.0 / float(Lc), dtype=np.float64)
    e /= s
    return e


def grad_corr(q, p, A, N, n):
    Q = rfft(q, N)
    g = irfft(Q * np.conjugate(A), N)
    g = g[:n].copy()
    np.clip(g, 0.0, None, out=g)
    g *= 2.0
    return g


def center_scale(h):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return None
    h = h - float(h.mean())
    hmax = float(h.max())
    hmin = float(h.min())
    rng = hmax - hmin
    if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng <= 1e-18:
        return None
    out = h / (0.5 * rng)
    np.clip(out, -5.0, 5.0, out=out)
    return out


def scale_normalize(p, h, eta):
    if eta <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    eh = -eta * h
    np.clip(eh, -700.0, 700.0, out=eh)
    f = np.exp(eh)
    p2 = p * f
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    p2 /= s
    return p2


def perturb_sequence(p, sigma, rng):
    if sigma <= 0.0:
        return normalize_pos(p, len(p))
    p = p * np.exp(sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, p.size))
    return normalize_pos(p, p.size)


def trial_accepts(m_new, v_new, m_cur, v_cur, allow_peak):
    if m_new < m_cur - 1e-15:
        return True
    if allow_peak > 0.0:
        peak_ok = m_new <= m_cur * (1.0 + float(allow_peak))
        var_ok = False
        if v_new < v_cur - 1e-18:
            var_ok = True
        elif v_cur > 0.0 and v_new < v_cur * (1.0 - 1e-8):
            var_ok = True
        if peak_ok and var_ok:
            return True
    return False


def trial_better(m1, v1, m2, v2):
    if m1 < m2 - 1e-15:
        return True
    if m2 < m1 - 1e-15:
        return False
    return v1 < v2


def generate_initials(n, rng, prev):
    base = make_base(n)
    rev = base[::-1].copy()
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    cands = []
    cands.append(base)
    cands.append(rev)

    cands.append(base * np.exp(-0.4 * x))
    cands.append(base * np.exp(0.4 * x))
    cands.append(rev * np.exp(-0.4 * x))
    cands.append(rev * np.exp(0.4 * x))

    cands.append(base * (1.0 + 0.3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * x + 0.4)))
    cands.append(base * (1.0 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 4.0 * x)))
    cands.append(base * (1.0 + 0.3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 8.0 * x + 1.2)))

    cands.append(base * np.exp(0.20 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)))

    if prev is not None and prev.size >= 16:
        cands.append(resample_sequence(prev, n))

    return cands


def optimize_sequence(p_init, n, N, Lc, time_budget, T_frac,
                      eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng, tag):
    p = normalize_pos(p_init, n)
    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    start = time.monotonic()

    c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p, N, Lc)
    cur_var = compute_var(c, Lc)

    best_seq = p.copy()
    best_max = cur_max
    best_var = cur_var
    best_F = 2.0 * float(n) * best_max

    eta = float(eta_init)
    if eta > float(max_eta):
        eta = float(max_eta)
    if eta < float(min_eta):
        eta = float(min_eta)

    fail = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_budget:
        it += 1
        if it % 750 == 0:
            print(tag, "iter=%d F=%.8f eta=%.5g fail=%d" % (it, best_F, eta, fail))

        if best_max > 0.0 and cur_max > best_max * 1.03 + 1e-15:
            p = best_seq.copy()
            c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p, N, Lc)
            cur_var = compute_var(c, Lc)
            fail = 0

        q = make_q(c, T_frac, Lc)
        G = grad_corr(q, p, A, N, n)
        h = center_scale(G)

        if h is None:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 80:
                break
            p = perturb_sequence(best_seq, 0.08, rng)
            c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p, N, Lc)
            cur_var = compute_var(c, Lc)
            if cur_max < best_max:
                best_max = cur_max
                best_seq = p.copy()
                best_var = cur_var
                best_F = 2.0 * float(n) * best_max
            eta = max(float(min_eta), min(float(max_eta), float(eta_init) * 0.70))
            fail = 0
            continue

        eta_full = min(eta, float(max_eta))
        if eta_full < float(min_eta):
            break

        accepted = False
        best_m = None
        best_v = None
        best_p_try = None
        best_A_try = None
        best_c_try = None
        best_fact = 1.0

        p_try = scale_normalize(p, h, eta_full)
        if p_try is not None:
            m_try, v_try, A_try, c_try = eval_max_var(p_try, N, Lc)
            full_ok = trial_accepts(m_try, v_try, cur_max, cur_var, allow_peak)

            if full_ok:
                accepted = True
                best_m = m_try
                best_v = v_try
                best_p_try = p_try
                best_A_try = A_try
                best_c_try = c_try
                best_fact = 1.0

                improve = cur_max - m_try
                if m_try < cur_max and improve <= 1e-8 * max(cur_max, 1e-300):
                    factors = (0.5, 0.25, 0.125)
                else:
                    factors = ()
            else:
                factors = (0.5, 0.25, 0.125)

            for fact in factors:
                e = eta_full * float(fact)
                if e < float(min_eta):
                    continue
                pt2 = scale_normalize(p, h, e)
                if pt2 is None:
                    continue
                m2, v2, A2, c2 = eval_max_var(pt2, N, Lc)
                if trial_accepts(m2, v2, cur_max, cur_var, allow_peak):
                    if (not accepted) or trial_better(m2, v2, best_m, best_v):
                        accepted = True
                        best_m = m2
                        best_v = v2
                        best_p_try = pt2
                        best_A_try = A2
                        best_c_try = c2
                        best_fact = float(fact)

        if accepted and best_p_try is not None:
            p = best_p_try
            cur_max = best_m
            cur_var = best_v
            A = best_A_try
            c = best_c_try
            fail = 0

            if cur_max < best_max - 1e-18:
                best_max = cur_max
                best_seq = p.copy()
                best_var = cur_var
                best_F = 2.0 * float(n) * best_max

            if best_fact > 0.9:
                eta = min(float(max_eta), eta * 1.06)
            elif best_fact > 0.4:
                eta = max(float(min_eta), eta * 0.98)
            else:
                eta = max(float(min_eta), eta * 0.85)
        else:
            fail += 1
            eta = max(float(min_eta), eta * 0.72)

            if fail > 80:
                p = perturb_sequence(best_seq, 0.07, rng)
                c, A, cur_max = eval_main(p, N, Lc)
                cur_var = compute_var(c, Lc)
                if cur_max < best_max:
                    best_max = cur_max
                    best_seq = p.copy()
                    best_var = cur_var
                    best_F = 2.0 * float(n) * best_max
                eta = max(float(min_eta), min(float(max_eta), float(eta_init) * 0.75))
                fail = 0

            if eta <= float(min_eta) * 1.02 and fail > 30:
                break
            if fail > 1500:
                break

    return best_seq, best_F


def run_stage(p, n, N, Lc, deadline_stage, T_frac, eta_init, min_eta,
              max_eta, allow_peak, rng, tag):
    tl = float(deadline_stage) - time.monotonic()
    if tl < 2.0:
        return p, 2.0 * float(n) * eval_max_only(p, N, Lc)
    return optimize_sequence(p, n, N, Lc, tl, T_frac,
                             eta_init, min_eta, max_eta, allow_peak, rng, tag)


def search_length(n, deadline, rng, prev):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = float(deadline)
    if deadline <= start + 30.0:
        return None, np.inf

    budget = deadline - start
    N = next_fft_len_for_n(n)
    Lc = 2 * int(n) - 1

    inits = generate_initials(n, rng, prev)

    p_list = []
    F_list = []
    for init in inits:
        p = normalize_pos(init, n)
        maxc = eval_max_only(p, N, Lc)
        F = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
        p_list.append(p)
        F_list.append(F)

    if not p_list:
        return None, np.inf

    F_arr = np.asarray(F_list, dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(F_arr)
    top1_idx = int(order[0])
    top2_idx = int(order[1]) if len(order) > 1 else -1

    local_p = p_list[top1_idx].copy()
    local_F = float(F_list[top1_idx])
    print("len=%d best initial F=%.8f" % (n, local_F))

    min_eta = 1e-5

    if top2_idx >= 0:
        d1 = start + 0.14 * budget
        d2 = start + 0.30 * budget
        d3 = start + 0.52 * budget
        start2 = start + 0.55 * budget
    else:
        d1 = start + 0.18 * budget
        d2 = start + 0.36 * budget
        d3 = start + 0.62 * budget
        start2 = start + 0.65 * budget

    p = local_p

    p, F = run_stage(p, n, N, Lc, d1, 0.22, 0.70, min_eta, 1.60, 0.010, rng,
                     "len%d_s1" % int(n))
    if F < local_F:
        local_p, local_F = p, F

    p, F = run_stage(p, n, N, Lc, d2, 0.12, 0.45, min_eta, 1.20, 0.005, rng,
                     "len%d_s2" % int(n))
    if F < local_F:
        local_p, local_F = p, F

    p, F = run_stage(p, n, N, Lc, d3, 0.05, 0.28, min_eta, 0.90, 0.002, rng,
                     "len%d_s3" % int(n))
    if F < local_F:
        local_p, local_F = p, F

    if start2 < deadline - 30.0:
        use_top2 = (top2_idx >= 0) and (float(F_list[top2_idx]) <= local_F * 1.10)
        if use_top2:
            p_base = p_list[top2_idx].copy()
        else:
            p_base = perturb_sequence(local_p, 0.06, rng)

        mid = start2 + 0.5 * (deadline - start2)

        p, F = run_stage(p_base, n, N, Lc, mid, 0.15, 0.50, min_eta, 1.10, 0.010, rng,
                         "len%d_s4" % int(n))
        if F < local_F:
            local_p, local_F = p, F

        p, F = run_stage(p, n, N, Lc, deadline, 0.05, 0.30, min_eta, 0.80, 0.002, rng,
                         "len%d_s5" % int(n))
        if F < local_F:
            local_p, local_F = p, F

    print("len=%d search best F=%.8f" % (n, local_F))
    return local_p, local_F


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=None):
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 2300.0
    budget_s = float(budget_s)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    horizon = min(budget_s, 2360.0)
    hard_deadline = t0 + horizon - 30.0
    if hard_deadline < t0 + 20.0:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, horizon * 0.5)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_p = None
    best_F = np.inf

    prev = get_previous_sequence()

    if prev is not None and prev.size >= 16:
        try:
            n0 = int(prev.size)
            p0 = normalize_pos(prev, n0)
            N0 = next_fft_len_for_n(n0)
            L0 = 2 * n0 - 1
            F0 = 2.0 * float(n0) * eval_max_only(p0, N0, L0)
            if np.isfinite(F0) and F0 < best_F:
                best_p = p0
                best_F = F0
            print("previous sequence F=%.8f" % F0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0:
        dead8192 = time.monotonic() + 0.60 * rem
        p8, F8 = search_length(8192, dead8192, rng, prev)
        if p8 is not None and F8 < best_F:
            best_p, best_F = p8, F8
            print("after 8192 F=%.8f" % best_F)

    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 500.0:
        p16, F16 = search_length(16384, hard_deadline - 20.0, rng, prev)
        if p16 is not None and F16 < best_F:
            best_p, best_F = p16, F16
            print("after 16384 F=%.8f" % best_F)

    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_p is not None:
        n = int(best_p.size)
        N = next_fft_len_for_n(n)
        Lc = 2 * n - 1
        min_eta = 1e-5
        mid = time.monotonic() + 0.55 * rem

        p_pol, F_pol = run_stage(best_p, n, N, Lc, mid, 0.05, 0.20, min_eta, 0.65,
                                 0.002, rng, "final1")
        if F_pol < best_F:
            best_p, best_F = p_pol, F_pol

        p_pol, F_pol = run_stage(best_p, n, N, Lc, hard_deadline - 8.0, 0.02, 0.12,
                                 min_eta, 0.45, 0.0, rng, "final2")
        if F_pol < best_F:
            best_p, best_F = p_pol, F_pol

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = make_base(8192)
        n = int(best_p.size)
        N = next_fft_len_for_n(n)
        Lc = 2 * n - 1
        best_F = 2.0 * float(n) * eval_max_only(best_p, N, Lc)

    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if (ev is not None and best_p is not None and len(best_p) <= 10000 and
            time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 8.0):
        try:
            val = ev([float(x) for x in best_p])
            if np.isfinite(val):
                best_F = float(val)
            print("official evaluate check F=%.8f" % best_F)
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_p = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    best_p = np.where(np.isfinite(best_p), best_p, 0.0)
    np.clip(best_p, 0.0, 1000.0, out=best_p)
    s = float(best_p.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        best_p = make_base(8192)

    print("returned length=%d best_F=%.8f" % (best_p.size, best_F))
    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```
